93                          back_reg_push |-> (fwd_count > push_count))
94                          else $error("tile_cmte: word pushed into back_reg with no read forward (fwd=%0d push=%0d)",

97                        seam_one_word_forwarded: assert property (@(posedge clk_i) disable iff (reset_i || clear_i)
98                          fwd_count <= push_count + 1)

101                     
102                       // ---- Deadlock signature: a second word waiting for its handoff ----
103                       // A command is stalled behind the seam (a DESC/SETC behind an armed word,
104                       // or ENDC behind back_reg), back_reg's word is still waiting for its
105                       // datapath handoff, and no handoff is being presented. A sequential
106                       // producer cannot reach that word's LD b=1 past the stalled command, so
107                       // the engine is stuck for good: the program broke the one-word-pending
108                       // rule (INSTRUCTION_REFERENCE LD) or dropped an LD b=1. The window only
109                       // absorbs producer pipeline latency between executing an LD and raising
110                       // dp_valid_i; a legal program never holds this signature at all.
111                       localparam int unsigned HANDOFF_STALL_LIMIT = 256;
112                     
113                       logic        handoff_stall;
114                       int unsigned handoff_stall_cycles;
115                       assign handoff_stall = valid_i && !ready_o && !back_reg_free && !dp_valid_i &&
116                                              (front_end_u.armed_r || (cmte_cmd_i.command == CMTE_CMD_ENDC));
117                     
118                       always @(posedge clk_i) begin
119        2/2              if (reset_i || clear_i || !handoff_stall) handoff_stall_cycles <= 0;
120        0/1     ==>      else                                      handoff_stall_cycles <= handoff_stall_cycles + 1;

153                       // wb_free is WB's own occupancy flag, declared here (not in the WB
154                       // section below) because wr_to_wb_pop is the first place that needs it;
155                       // be_drained (ENDC section) is its other consumer. Same pattern as
156                       // wr_free above, one stage over.
157                       logic wr_to_wb_pop, wr_discard, wb_free;
158                     
159                       assign wb_free      = !wb_valid_r;
160                       assign wr_to_wb_pop = wr_valid_r && wr_merged_r && (|wr_wmask_r) && wb_free;

262                       // ================= Delta =================
263                       // ----------------- ENDC / quiescence -----------------
264                       // be_drained: RB/WR/WB/skid all empty -- purely structural, the point
265                       // past which no further commit_valid_i can land (wr_free here is
266                       // mutually exclusive with consume's wr_valid_r). Only meaningful as the
267                       // ENDC_PENDING -> ENDC_SENT transition condition below.
268                       logic be_drained;
269                       assign be_drained = rb_empty && skid_free && wr_free && wb_free;
270                     
271                       // ship_u2d: one-shot pulse to delta, firing exactly once on the
272                       // ENDC_PENDING -> ENDC_SENT edge, telling it the accumulator is final
273                       // and safe to snapshot. delta owns the rest of the handoff itself:
274                       // delta_valid_o holds from the ship until delta_ready_i takes it, and
275                       // delta_fire is that transfer, which is what ENDC_SENT waits for.
276                       logic ship_u2d, delta_fire;
277                       assign ship_u2d   = (endc_state_r == ENDC_PENDING) && be_drained;
278                       assign delta_fire = delta_valid_o && delta_ready_i;

284                         .cell_bounds_i(back_reg_r.cell_bounds), .base_class_i(back_reg_r.base_class),

292        1/1              if (reset_i || clear_i) begin
293        1/1                endc_state_r <= ENDC_IDLE;
294                         end else begin
295        1/1                unique case (endc_state_r)
296        2/2                  ENDC_IDLE:     if (endc_i)         endc_state_r <= ENDC_PENDING;

                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
299        1/1                  PROG_COMPLETE: ; // holds until clear_i

304                       // program_done_o is purely registered: it rises the cycle after the Delta
305                       // transfer (by then the consumer has already taken delta_o) and holds,
306                       // sticky, until clear_i. Nothing here depends combinationally on
307                       // delta_ready_i.
308                       assign program_done_o = (endc_state_r == PROG_COMPLETE);
309                     
310                     `ifndef SYNTHESIS
311                       // Simulation-only checks are excluded by the SYNTHESIS define.
312                     
313                       // clear_i may start once the image has completed, or while the back end
314                       // holds nothing (e.g. the tile's first dispatch after reset). Only the
315                       // rising edge is checked -- clear itself resets the state that proves it.
316                       // tile_cmte checks the front end's half.
317                       clear_needs_quiescent_be: assert property (@(posedge clk_i) disable iff (reset_i)
318                         $rose(clear_i) |-> (program_done_o ||
319                                             ((endc_state_r == ENDC_IDLE) && be_drained && !back_reg_valid_r)))
320                         else $error("back_end: clear_i while the back end still holds work");
321                     
322                       // consume requires wr_valid_r, ship_u2d requires wr_free -- they can
323                       // never coincide, so delta_acc_r never changes out from under the
324                       // snapshot delta takes of it.
325                       commit_ship_mutex: assert property (@(posedge clk_i) disable iff (reset_i)
326                         !(consume && ship_u2d))
327                         else $error("back_end: commit landed the same cycle ship_u2d fired");
328                     
329                       // ---- Front-end sequencing the back end relies on ----
330                       be_push_into_free_back_reg: assert property (@(posedge clk_i) disable iff (reset_i || clear_i)
331                         back_reg_push_i |-> back_reg_free_o)
332                         else $error("back_end: back_reg push while back_reg still holds a word");
333                     
334                       // RB + skid are only safe because the front end never forwards more than
335                       // one word ahead of the seam. A forward that isn't taken is lost; one that
336                       // lands in RB past a waiting skid entry reorders words.
337                       be_fwd_never_dropped: assert property (@(posedge clk_i) disable iff (reset_i || clear_i)
338                         read_fwd_valid_i |-> read_fwd_ready_o)
339                         else $error("back_end: read forward dropped (RB and skid both full)");
340                     
341                       be_fwd_keeps_order: assert property (@(posedge clk_i) disable iff (reset_i || clear_i)
342                         !(read_fwd_valid_i && rb_empty && skid_valid_r))
343                         else $error("back_end: read forward overtook the skid entry (word order lost)");
344                     
345                       be_endc_after_seam_drained: assert property (@(posedge clk_i) disable iff (reset_i || clear_i)
346                         endc_i |-> !back_reg_valid_r)
347                         else $error("back_end: ENDC while back_reg still holds a word");
348                     
349                       // ---- Datapath handoff (F9): the producer holds A/R31 until dp_ack_o ----
350                       be_dp_holds_until_ack: assert property (@(posedge clk_i) disable iff (reset_i || clear_i)
351                         (dp_valid_i && !dp_ack_o) |=> (dp_valid_i && $stable(accumulator_i) && $stable(guards_i)))
352                         else $error("back_end: dp_valid_i/accumulator_i/guards_i changed before dp_ack_o");
353                     
354                       // ---- VS response contract: a read's rsp arrives >= 1 cycle after its ack ----
355                       be_rsp_only_when_waiting: assert property (@(posedge clk_i) disable iff (reset_i || clear_i)
356                         vs_bus.rsp_valid |-> (rb_state_r == RB_WAIT_RESP))
357                         else $error("back_end: VS rsp_valid while RB is not waiting for one (response lost)");
358                     
359                       be_done_sticky: assert property (@(posedge clk_i) disable iff (reset_i)
360                         (program_done_o && !clear_i) |=> program_done_o)
361                         else $error("back_end: program_done_o dropped before clear_i");
362                     
363                       // ---- Each value-store bit is committed at most once per image ----
364                       // A second commit of a bit can take its old value from a read issued
365                       // before the first commit's write lands, so it compares against stale data
366                       // and silently drops its own update. The ISA makes that program malformed;
367                       // this shadow of every bit committed since reset/clear catches it.
368                       logic [WORD_WIDTH-1:0] committed_bits [logic [WIDX-1:0]];
369                       always @(posedge clk_i) begin
370        1/1              if (reset_i || clear_i) begin
371        1/1                committed_bits.delete();
372        1/1              end else if (consume) begin
373        1/1                if (committed_bits.exists(wr_addr_r)) begin
374                             be_bit_committed_once: assert ((committed_bits[wr_addr_r] & back_reg_r.field) == '0)
375                               else $error("back_end: word %h commits bits %h a second time this image (malformed)",
376                                           {wr_addr_r, 2'b00}, committed_bits[wr_addr_r] & back_reg_r.field);
377        1/1                  committed_bits[wr_addr_r] = committed_bits[wr_addr_r] | back_reg_r.field;
378                           end else begin
379        1/1                  committed_bits[wr_addr_r] = back_reg_r.field;
380                           end
381                         end

241                       // ================= VSP =================
242                     
243                       // RB wants the port for as long as it's WAIT_ACK -- ack alone doesn't
244                       // resolve a read, so state moves to RB_WAIT_RESP
245                       logic  vs_want_read, vs_want_write;
246                       assign vs_want_read = (rb_state_r == RB_WAIT_ACK);
247                       assign vs_want_write = wb_valid_r; // WB wants port when it holds a write
248                     
249                       vsp vsp_u (
250                         .clk_i(clk_i), .reset_i(reset_i), .clear_i(clear_i),
251                     
252                         .vs_want_read_i(vs_want_read), .vs_want_write_i(vs_want_write),
253                     
254                         .rb_addr_i(rb_addr_r), .wb_addr_i(wb_addr_r),
255                         .wb_data_i(wb_data_r), .wb_wmask_i(wb_wmask_r),
256                     
257                         .read_ack_o(vsp_read_ack), .write_ack_o(vsp_write_ack),
258                     
259                         .vs_bus(vs_bus)
260                       );
261                     
262                       // ================= Delta =================
263                       // ----------------- ENDC / quiescence -----------------
264                       // be_drained: RB/WR/WB/skid all empty -- purely structural, the point
265                       // past which no further commit_valid_i can land (wr_free here is
266                       // mutually exclusive with consume's wr_valid_r). Only meaningful as the
267                       // ENDC_PENDING -> ENDC_SENT transition condition below.
268                       logic be_drained;
269                       assign be_drained = rb_empty && skid_free && wr_free && wb_free;
270                     
271                       // ship_u2d: one-shot pulse to delta, firing exactly once on the
272                       // ENDC_PENDING -> ENDC_SENT edge, telling it the accumulator is final
273                       // and safe to snapshot. delta owns the rest of the handoff itself:
274                       // delta_valid_o holds from the ship until delta_ready_i takes it, and
275                       // delta_fire is that transfer, which is what ENDC_SENT waits for.
276                       logic ship_u2d, delta_fire;
277                       assign ship_u2d   = (endc_state_r == ENDC_PENDING) && be_drained;
278                       assign delta_fire = delta_valid_o && delta_ready_i;
279                     
280                       delta delta_u (
281                         .clk_i(clk_i), .reset_i(reset_i), .clear_i(clear_i),
282                     
283                         .commit_valid_i(consume), .dirty_i(dirty),
284                         .cell_bounds_i(back_reg_r.cell_bounds), .base_class_i(back_reg_r.base_class),
285                     
286                         .ship_i(ship_u2d), .delta_ready_i(delta_ready_i),
287                     
288                         .delta_o(delta_o), .delta_valid_o(delta_valid_o)
289                       );
290                     
291                       always_ff @(posedge clk_i) begin
292        1/1              if (reset_i || clear_i) begin
293        1/1                endc_state_r <= ENDC_IDLE;
294                         end else begin
295        1/1                unique case (endc_state_r)
296        2/2                  ENDC_IDLE:     if (endc_i)         endc_state_r <= ENDC_PENDING;
                        MISSING_ELSE
297        2/2                  ENDC_PENDING:  if (be_drained)     endc_state_r <= ENDC_SENT;
                        MISSING_ELSE
298        2/2                  ENDC_SENT:     if (delta_fire)     endc_state_r <= PROG_COMPLETE;
                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
299        1/1                  PROG_COMPLETE: ; // holds until clear_i
300                           endcase
301                         end
302                       end
303                     
304                       // program_done_o is purely registered: it rises the cycle after the Delta
305                       // transfer (by then the consumer has already taken delta_o) and holds,
306                       // sticky, until clear_i. Nothing here depends combinationally on
307                       // delta_ready_i.
308                       assign program_done_o = (endc_state_r == PROG_COMPLETE);
309                     
310                     `ifndef SYNTHESIS
311                       // Simulation-only checks are excluded by the SYNTHESIS define.
312                     
313                       // clear_i may start once the image has completed, or while the back end
314                       // holds nothing (e.g. the tile's first dispatch after reset). Only the
315                       // rising edge is checked -- clear itself resets the state that proves it.
316                       // tile_cmte checks the front end's half.
317                       clear_needs_quiescent_be: assert property (@(posedge clk_i) disable iff (reset_i)
318                         $rose(clear_i) |-> (program_done_o ||
319                                             ((endc_state_r == ENDC_IDLE) && be_drained && !back_reg_valid_r)))
320                         else $error("back_end: clear_i while the back end still holds work");
321                     
322                       // consume requires wr_valid_r, ship_u2d requires wr_free -- they can
323                       // never coincide, so delta_acc_r never changes out from under the
324                       // snapshot delta takes of it.
325                       commit_ship_mutex: assert property (@(posedge clk_i) disable iff (reset_i)
326                         !(consume && ship_u2d))
327                         else $error("back_end: commit landed the same cycle ship_u2d fired");
328                     
329                       // ---- Front-end sequencing the back end relies on ----
330                       be_push_into_free_back_reg: assert property (@(posedge clk_i) disable iff (reset_i || clear_i)
331                         back_reg_push_i |-> back_reg_free_o)
332                         else $error("back_end: back_reg push while back_reg still holds a word");
333                     
334                       // RB + skid are only safe because the front end never forwards more than
335                       // one word ahead of the seam. A forward that isn't taken is lost; one that
336                       // lands in RB past a waiting skid entry reorders words.
337                       be_fwd_never_dropped: assert property (@(posedge clk_i) disable iff (reset_i || clear_i)
338                         read_fwd_valid_i |-> read_fwd_ready_o)
339                         else $error("back_end: read forward dropped (RB and skid both full)");
340                     
341                       be_fwd_keeps_order: assert property (@(posedge clk_i) disable iff (reset_i || clear_i)
342                         !(read_fwd_valid_i && rb_empty && skid_valid_r))
343                         else $error("back_end: read forward overtook the skid entry (word order lost)");
344                     
345                       be_endc_after_seam_drained: assert property (@(posedge clk_i) disable iff (reset_i || clear_i)
346                         endc_i |-> !back_reg_valid_r)
347                         else $error("back_end: ENDC while back_reg still holds a word");
348                     
349                       // ---- Datapath handoff (F9): the producer holds A/R31 until dp_ack_o ----
350                       be_dp_holds_until_ack: assert property (@(posedge clk_i) disable iff (reset_i || clear_i)
351                         (dp_valid_i && !dp_ack_o) |=> (dp_valid_i && $stable(accumulator_i) && $stable(guards_i)))
352                         else $error("back_end: dp_valid_i/accumulator_i/guards_i changed before dp_ack_o");
353                     
354                       // ---- VS response contract: a read's rsp arrives >= 1 cycle after its ack ----
355                       be_rsp_only_when_waiting: assert property (@(posedge clk_i) disable iff (reset_i || clear_i)
356                         vs_bus.rsp_valid |-> (rb_state_r == RB_WAIT_RESP))
357                         else $error("back_end: VS rsp_valid while RB is not waiting for one (response lost)");
358                     
359                       be_done_sticky: assert property (@(posedge clk_i) disable iff (reset_i)
360                         (program_done_o && !clear_i) |=> program_done_o)
361                         else $error("back_end: program_done_o dropped before clear_i");
362                     
363                       // ---- Each value-store bit is committed at most once per image ----
364                       // A second commit of a bit can take its old value from a read issued
365                       // before the first commit's write lands, so it compares against stale data
366                       // and silently drops its own update. The ISA makes that program malformed;
367                       // this shadow of every bit committed since reset/clear catches it.
368                       logic [WORD_WIDTH-1:0] committed_bits [logic [WIDX-1:0]];
369                       always @(posedge clk_i) begin
370        1/1              if (reset_i || clear_i) begin
371        1/1                committed_bits.delete();
372        1/1              end else if (consume) begin
373        1/1                if (committed_bits.exists(wr_addr_r)) begin
374                             be_bit_committed_once: assert ((committed_bits[wr_addr_r] & back_reg_r.field) == '0)
375                               else $error("back_end: word %h commits bits %h a second time this image (malformed)",
376                                           {wr_addr_r, 2'b00}, committed_bits[wr_addr_r] & back_reg_r.field);
377        1/1                  committed_bits[wr_addr_r] = committed_bits[wr_addr_r] | back_reg_r.field;
378                           end else begin
379        1/1                  committed_bits[wr_addr_r] = back_reg_r.field;
380                           end
381                         end
                        MISSING_ELSE

131                       // ================= paint action dispatch =================
132                       // The "accepted command" and "push" groups never coincide the same cycle:
133                       // every accepted DESC and SETC needs armed_r=0, and push needs armed_r=1.
134                       always_comb begin
135        1/1              if (is_setc && ready_o)
136        1/1                paint_action = PAINT_SETC;
137        1/1              else if (is_desc && ready_o && !cmd_i.wide)
138        1/1                paint_action = PAINT_NARROW;
139        1/1              else if (is_desc && ready_o && cmd_i.wide)
140        1/1                paint_action = PAINT_WIDE_FIRST;
141        1/1              else if (push && wide_continues && !wide_entering_final)
142        1/1                paint_action = PAINT_HOLD;
143        1/1              else if (push && wide_entering_final)
144        1/1                paint_action = PAINT_WIDE_FINAL;
145        1/1              else if (push && !wide_continues)
146        1/1                paint_action = PAINT_CLEAR;
147                         else

182                               // last_bits_m1 all-ones -> last_bits=32: the trailing limb is
183                               // full-width, so nothing can ever be packed after it -- it must
